## INNER LOOP -- live directives the running loop applies each lap (seated `20260816.214652`)

*The outer shell loop reads this card first every lap, so directives here take effect on the NEXT lap without a restart. The agent MAY edit this block as it goes -- it is the inner loop the outer loop points at. Keep it ASCII, short, and current; move settled items down into the body.*

- **ASCII-first.** Write every new document, comment, and commit message in plain ASCII. No em-dashes, middots, curly quotes, arrows, or non-ASCII math -- use `--`, `-`, `'`, `"`, `->`, `<=`, `gamma_2`. The one exception is an explicitly-named set of work rounds (e.g. a Unicode-handling module's own fixtures). Rule: `.claude/rules/ascii-first.md`. This card was corrupted to mojibake once (REDS #83); ASCII-first is how it never happens again.
- **Spelling: American.** Use `color`, not `colour`; normalize `colour -> color` on touch. This is a USA project.
- **Style sweep before every send** -- Radiant pass over the round's prose (Twilight for a night piece), register only never a claim. Seed section 6.
- **Rota of the canon.** Each lap, read ONE doc from the 27-doc council rota in `recursion-prompts/seed/autonomous-loop.seed.md` section 1, cycling by lap index, so the durable foundations and context stay in awareness across the run.

Next open-media: the uppercase companions to finish Latin-1 capitals (A ring U+00C5, AE U+00C6, O stroke U+00D8, Eth U+00D0, Thorn U+00DE, Y acute U+00DD); the captioned panel proven on longer/varied real reels; the open glyph atlas beyond 5x7. **`image/glyph_compose.rye` GREEN** -- the combining-diacritic overlay `overlay(base, mark, cell_area)` (ink union over the coverage grid): `overlay(lowercase 'a', grave mark)` reproduces the hand-drawn `a grave` byte-for-byte, and the same for e/o/u, proving the accent grind is composition -- O(letters x accents) collapses to O(letters + accents); commutative, blank-mark identity, refusals named (BadCellArea/BaseLenMismatch/MarkLenMismatch). **`image/glyph_accent.rye` GREEN** -- the overlay's payoff: `compose_accent_atlas(base_atlas, mark, map, advance)` grows a WHOLE accented corpus by overlaying one mark onto each mapped base glyph; proven on metal that composing the lowercase base with the grave mark over the a/e/o/u grave map reproduces the hand-drawn `font5x7_grave` coverage byte-for-byte AND the composed atlas round-trips through gfa1 byte-identical, so a corpus is now a table plus one mark, not a hand of drawings (map required strictly ascending; BaseGlyphMissing/MarkLenMismatch/MapNotAscending/EmptyMap/TooManyAccents refuse by name). **`image/font5x7_acute.rye` GREEN** -- the first accent corpus GROWN by composition rather than drawn: the acute mark drawn once, the a/e/o/u acute set (U+00E1,U+00E9,U+00F3,U+00FA) grown by `compose_accent_atlas` from the lowercase base plus a four-row table, each byte-for-byte the hand-drawn `font5x7_latin1` acute coverage, round-tripping through gfa1 byte-identical, every mark proven leaning right (mirror of the grave), the dotted i acute honestly omitted (its base carries a dot the mark would not replace). The economy is now proven on a second mark. And `image/font5x7_upper_nordic.rye` -- the open Danish-Norwegian CAPITALS corpus (A ring U+00C5, AE ligature U+00C6, O stroke U+00D8), the uppercase companion to font5x7_nordic -- is GREEN: the three capitals a civic surface prints at the head of a name or in ALL-CAPS (AERO, BJORN, KOBENHAVN), each compressed into rows 2..6 the way the accented capitals are, leaving rows 0..1 for a mark; the crux mirrors its lowercase twin -- THREE DISTINCT CONSTRUCTIONS each read by its own toothed guard (A ring hollow ring above, AE ligature mark-less with a full crossbar, O stroke inking the bowl center), round-trip byte-identical, present, distinct, legible (ink 15..19 of 35). Honest scope: this closes the Scandinavian capitals, not the whole uppercase Latin-1 set -- the Icelandic capitals (Eth U+00D0, Thorn U+00DE, Y acute U+00DD) remain, exactly as the lowercase Nordic corpus preceded the lowercase Icelandic. Next accent rungs draw circumflex/tilde/diaeresis marks once each and grow their whole sets from a table the same way, and the Icelandic capitals close the uppercase Latin-1 letter set outright. The whole Western European printable text now reads as ONE font lookup -- `image/font5x7_latin1_text.rye` is GREEN, merging the ASCII font (95 glyphs) and the Latin-1 block font (62) into one 157-glyph atlas, proving the ASCII half contiguous, the Latin-1 half block-complete, and the gap 0x7F..0xBF between them empty (the single font a surface loads for any Western European document). The whole Latin-1 letter block reads as ONE lookup too -- `image/font5x7_latin1_block.rye` is GREEN, the unifying Latin-1 letter font that gathers all thirteen leaf corpora into one gfa1 atlas (62 letters, U+00C0..U+00FF) and proves BLOCK COMPLETENESS: the whole 64-codepoint block present and carrying the shared advance except exactly the two non-letters (multiplication U+00D7, division U+00F7, which live in font5x7_math), the thirteen corpora disjoint, the count equal to the sum of the corpora AND the block's letter span. And `image/font5x7_arrows.rye` -- THE UNIFYING ARROW FONT, the FIRST unifier on the SYMBOL side of the family (sibling to font5x7_ascii and font5x7_latin1_block, neither of which holds a symbol) -- is GREEN: it merges the two arrow corpora (font5x7_arrow's six basic directions U+2190..U+2195 and font5x7_arrowdiag's four diagonals U+2196..U+2199) into ONE gfa1 atlas of the whole eight-direction arrow set. Its crux is CONTIGUOUS block completeness, stronger than the Latin-1 unifier's two-hole block: the arrow span carries NO hole, so the selftest walks the whole ten-codepoint span and proves every codepoint present, the run unbroken, the merged count (6+4=10) equal to the span itself. And `image/font5x7_boxdrawing.rye` -- THE UNIFYING BOX-DRAWING FONT, the SECOND unifier on the symbol side -- is GREEN: it merges the four box corpora (font5x7_box's 11 light marks U+2500..U+253C, font5x7_boxdouble's 11 double twins U+2550..U+256C, font5x7_boxarc's 4 rounded corners U+256D..U+2570, font5x7_boxdiag's 3 diagonals U+2571..U+2573) into ONE gfa1 atlas of 29 marks -- the terminal frame set in a single lookup. Its crux is SPARSE block completeness, the deliberate contrast to the arrow font's contiguous span: the Box Drawing block is holey (29 marks across a span of 116 codepoints), so the selftest walks the whole span and proves EXACTLY 29 hits and 87 misses, spot-checking two named holes (heavy horizontal U+2501, heavy vertical U+2503) absent, so a phantom mark or dropped corner breaks the tally; count 11+11+4+3=29 with strictly-ascending codepoints proves the four corpora disjoint. The terminal family's WRITE loop is closed -- `pond/apps/scooter_compose.rye` is GREEN, the Season G rung where input meets the drawn face: a `Session` holds a channel (by pointer), the editable `dexter_line`, and the typing point; `open` enforces membership at the door, `submit` turns a typed line into a real membership-gated post (refusing an empty line by name, `EmptyEntry`) and clears the prompt, `frame` folds the whole session into one bounded 24x80 view (journal in the body via `scooter_view.render_within`, the prompt `> <line>` on the reserved last row, truncated with `~`), and `caret_col` tracks the caret for the later live TTY. Where `dexter_terminal` only DREW the journal above a prompt, this rung closes read+write -- Scooter is usable locally; the wire between two Constel piers reaches the serve gate. And `image/text_grid.rye` -- THE BRIDGE where the terminal grid meets the pixel family -- is GREEN: the two families grew side by side and never met on metal (the terminal grid holds a face as BYTES; the font/paint/qoi family paints PIXELS). `render_grid` paints a monospace grid of character bytes (the exact shape `scooter_view.Frame.cells` carries) into a real image, so a terminal face is not only bytes a reader parses but pixels a surface (Grainphone, Realidream, Skate) shows. Faithful BY MEASURE: the painted ink equals the summed glyph coverage of every non-space cell exactly, a blank row is ground byte-for-byte, a cell's pixel block equals a standalone glyph paint at its grid origin (`c*pitch, r*cell_h`); all-or-nothing box check (`GridOutOfBounds` by name), deterministic, round-trips through the open QOI codec byte-for-byte. Correctly layered -- `image/` composes only the image family; a pond rung feeds a live Frame's cells in one line later. And the coloured terminal is now LIVE -- `pond/apps/scooter_paint.rye` grows `render_channel_fg` / `paint_frame_fg`, painting a real Scooter conversation with the status header (row 0) in an accent colour and the body in the reading colour: a pure pass-through of `text_grid.render_grid_fg`, the coloured face inking the same pixels as the single-ink face (colour moves ink, adds none) yet a header pixel reading red where the body stays black, round-tripping through QOI byte-for-byte, a mis-sized palette refusing `GridFgMismatch` by name. And the coloured terminal CELL is now whole -- `text_grid.paint_grid_cells` / `render_grid_cells` give each cell its OWN ground beside its own ink (`fill_cell` writes the cell block directly, a uniform ground reproducing `render_grid_fg` pixel-for-pixel, a blue header bar leaving no band pixel white), so a header bar, a selected row, or an unread badge is a real coloured cell, round-tripping through QOI, a mis-sized bg refusing `GridBgMismatch` by name. And the coloured STATUS BAR is now LIVE -- `pond/apps/scooter_paint.rye` grows `render_channel_bar` / `paint_frame_cells`, painting a real Scooter conversation with its status row (row 0) as a real BAR: yellow ink over a BLUE header ground band, the body black over white, a pure pass-through of `text_grid.render_grid_cells` -- where the prior lap's `render_channel_fg` coloured only the header's LETTERS, this fills the whole header BAND, so no header pixel stays the old white page (proven by walking row 0: every pixel non-white, blue showing between the glyphs) and the body region is byte-for-byte identical to the single-ink face (the colouring confined to the status row), round-trips through QOI, a mis-sized bg refusing `GridBgMismatch` by name. And the DRAWN CARET is now LIVE -- `pond/apps/scooter_paint.rye` grows `render_session`, painting a composing `scooter_compose.Session` with a visible block CURSOR at the caret cell (inverse video, the classic terminal cursor): the caret cell reads a solid ink block where the plain painted session reads solid ground, the flip confined BYTE-FOR-BYTE to that one cell (values apart, never braided -- the conversation untouched), the cursor clearing and following the keeper when the caret walks home, round-tripping through QOI. The CURSOR PRIMITIVE now lives abstractly in `image/text_grid.rye` -- `render_grid_cursor` paints any grid plain but inverts one cell to a solid inverse-video block (a bad index refusing `GridCursorOutOfGrid` by name), reusable floor every grid surface shares; `render_session` is a pure pass-through of it, so the caret no longer lives inline in pond. And the KEYSTROKE DECODER is now GREEN -- `pond/apps/dexter_key.rye` turns a keeper's keyboard BYTES into bounded Key events and drives a `dexter_line`: `decode` is single-step (a printable byte, a control byte, or a whole ANSI escape each yield one key and consume 1+ bytes), `decode_escape` maps CSI/SS3 (C/D arrows, H/F edges, 3~ forward-delete, 1~/7~ Home, 4~/8~ End), `apply` runs dexter_line's proven API swallowing a boundary refusal to a harmless no-op, and `drive` loops to the first Enter so a caller submits between lines -- held PURE (a byte slice in, no raw TTY, no network), the eight cruxes proven on metal, unknown sequences ignored, deterministic. The raw TTY read (cbreak, escape timing) and the Comlink wire between two Constel piers reach the serve gate. **Git nib:** `12c4df2bf1` (living edge image/repeat_conic_paint.rye + tools/repeat_conic_paint_witness.rish GREEN, Season G open-media rung: A REPEATING CONIC POSITIONED GRADIENT SEEN ON A REAL IMAGE -- the SWEEP THAT TILES, the sixth and last shape of the CSS gradient family, closing the set the linear, radial, conic, repeating-linear, and repeating-radial rungs began. The conic twin of image/repeat_paint.rye and the repeating sibling of image/conic_paint.rye. Where the single conic gradient runs its ramp ONCE around the turn, paint_gradient_repeat_conic winds it reps times and TILES it (the CSS repeating-conic-gradient shape): reps identical pie wedges, a hard seam at each tile boundary, every ray from the center still one constant wedge color. Compose not braid over the same three public APIs -- color_ramp_stops DECIDES each pixel color, qoi owns the RGBA target, color composites src-over, this module only ROUTES each pixel to its tiled angular bearing. Routing needs no trigonometry: doubled coordinates (2*cx - (w-1)) keep the center exact, the bearing is held at FULL precision as the turn fraction T/D (T = q*s + numer, D = 4*s, s = |dx2| + |dy2|), and tiling winds and reduces it -- tile = (T*reps) mod D, scaled tile*pos_max/D. Holding T/D exact rather than the u8 bearing is the crux that lands the modulo on clean tile boundaries. Bounded: s <= ~16382, T < 4*s <= ~65528, reps <= qoi_max_dim, so T*reps <= ~5.4e8 and tile*pos_max <= ~1.7e7 stay far inside u64; box checked once up front in u64, reps checked against the dimension ceiling, ramp validated once, so a refusal paints nothing. PROVEN on metal (GREEN on the first build and first witness run): a black->white ramp on an odd 7x7 box, reps 2, center (3,3), makes color.mix return the tiled bearing in the red channel, so the top reads 0, east 127, south 0, west 127 (the ramp cycles twice around the turn, top and south sharing the first control where the single conic 0,63,127,191 never could), a cell just clockwise of the south seam reads 223 before south drops to 0 (a hard seam), reps 4 wraps the east bearing to 0 where reps 2 read 127 (the winding count a real control), a red-green-blue sweep round-trips through the open QOI codec byte-for-byte, a non-square 7x3 box still tops and wraps exactly; a single-pixel box refuses StripTooSmall, a zero dimension BadStripDims, a reps under two RepsTooSmall, a reps past the ceiling RepsTooLarge, an out-of-bounds box RepeatConicOutOfBounds, and a non-ascending ramp StopsNotAscending -- each having painted nothing; determinism byte-for-byte. tame_style_check bans clean, width-check exit 0, plain 7-bit ASCII. image/README.md names repeat_conic_paint.rye beside repeat_radial_paint.rye (docs-sync). Council rota lap 11 deep-read single-stranded.md. The six-member CSS gradient family is now whole in Rye. No network, no key, no funds.)

**Prior Git nib:** `b81c7c33c6` (living edge image/font5x7_boxdrawing.rye + tools/font5x7_boxdrawing_witness.rish GREEN, Season G open-media font content: THE UNIFYING BOX-DRAWING FONT -- the SECOND unifier on the symbol side of the 5x7 family, the sibling to the arrow font already landed (font5x7_arrows gathered the ten arrows) and to the two letter unifiers (font5x7_ascii the 95 printable ASCII glyphs, font5x7_latin1_block the 62 Latin-1 letters). The box-drawing marks still stood as four separate leaf corpora; this module gathers them whole. It merges font5x7_box (the 11 light marks, U+2500..U+253C), font5x7_boxdouble (their 11 double-line twins, U+2550..U+256C), font5x7_boxarc (the 4 rounded corners, U+256D..U+2570), and font5x7_boxdiag (the 3 diagonals, U+2571..U+2573) into ONE gfa1 atlas of 29 marks -- the whole terminal frame set a surface (the Dexter/Scooter family) reads in a single lookup the day it frames a panel, rules a table, or draws a soft or diagonal edge. THE CRUX beyond the round-trip and the ascending-and-unique disjointness the arrow unifier shares is SPARSE block completeness -- the deliberate contrast to the arrow set's contiguous span. The Box Drawing block is holey: 29 marks scattered across a span of 116 codepoints (U+2500..U+2573), the heavy-line variants not marks this open font draws. **Refinement `20260815.191048` -- Kumara crypto re-grounded (not "Lindy"), host updated, Puddle identities cleared, Brix in plan.** Keaton rightly rejected the Lindy metaphor for crypto (past != future when compute/quantum/AI-cryptanalysis all climb). The SLH-DSA choice **stands, re-justified structurally**: hashes have *no algebraic structure* for the rising attacks (Oct 2025 lattice sieving, CRYPTO 2026 sign-based key recovery, AI cryptanalysis) to exploit; quantum is only a Grover quadratic dent (256-bit output covers it); it adds *no new assumption* (Grain already relies on the hash everywhere -- seals, receipts, Merkle catalogs); and no mature *independent* family exists to hybridize with (code/multivariate broken, isogeny young, lattice eroding, symmetric-MPC not independent). **Confident. Parameter set: SLH-DSA-SHAKE-256s** (NIST cat 5). **Puddle cleared:** the agent may key + bootstrap **fake/test Kumara identities** in the constel/Puddle sandbox (consonant-only, non-@p-valid per placeholder-ship-names/FORA) -- the maintainer's *real* Kumara key stays the custody gate. **Brix seated in planning** (Brix config code + Brix docs molt, riding the docs-compression round). **Host updated** (GLOW_PROFILE): editor **Claude Code** (not Zed/Cursor), model claude-opus-4-8, host **Vultr SEA NixOS VPS**, hand **Daylight DC-1** (was Android Termux). No nixos-rebuild needed yet for the crypto rounds; will flag if SLH-DSA parity wants vendored FIPS 205 KAT vectors or a toolchain bump. Brief: [`../active-designing/20260815-184832_sha3-preference-and-post-quantum-kumara.md`](../active-designing/20260815-184832_sha3-preference-and-post-quantum-kumara.md).

**Kumara PQ SEATED `20260815.185922` -- SLH-DSA (SPHINCS+) on SHAKE-256, the most-Lindy path.** After an Aug 2026 news pass (Oct 2025 quantum-sieving cut the lattice-attack exponent ~8%; CRYPTO 2026 carries lattice-signature key-recovery papers), the century-root does not anchor to the assumption under active erosion. Kumara v1 signs **pure hash-based** -- security reduces to the hash alone, the oldest/most-Lindy primitive, industry's 30-year-guarantee tool -- making the SHA-3 preference load-bearing (identity is SHAKE-256). **Algorithm agility** seated: identity format carries a scheme tag + version so a second leg/migration accretes later (accrete-never-break for crypto). **Lattice co-signature deferred, not baked in** (revised my earlier SLH-DSA+ML-DSA hybrid lean on the evidence). Loop crux: SHA3-512/SHAKE-256 in Rye -> SLH-DSA build round, parity vs NIST FIPS 205 KATs; keygen/signing with the identity key stays the custody gate.

**Crypto policy `20260815.184832` -- SHA-3 preferred; Kumara goes post-quantum.** SEATED: prefer SHA-3/SHAKE over SHA-2 for new designs (structural defense-in-depth -- sponge, no length-extension -- *not* because SHA-2 is broken; SHA-512 stays for Monocypher/Ed25519 parity). Near-term no-gate crux: **SHA3-256/512 + SHAKE-256 in Rye**, parity vs Zig std.crypto + NIST FIPS 202 KATs (Monocypher has no SHA-3). CORRECTION seated: no elliptic curve is quantum-resistant (Shor breaks the curve regardless of hash); 512-bit hashes are already quantum-safe (Grover only halves -> ~256-bit). So Kumara's quantum answer is a **post-quantum signature, not a curve** -- and the conservative ones are hash-based, aligning with SHA-3. PROPOSED (wants Keaton's word): Kumara PQ scheme -- **A** SLH-DSA/SPHINCS+ (SHAKE-256, hash-based, most auditable) - **B** ML-DSA/Dilithium (SHAKE, fast, lattice) - **C** hybrid Ed25519+PQ. Breaking OpenSSH interop accepted. PROPOSED: a checkable security floor (refuse weaker primitives by named error) + root-README post-quantum promise + debride of weaker usage. Signing with the identity key stays the custody gate. Brief: [`../active-designing/20260815-184832_sha3-preference-and-post-quantum-kumara.md`](../active-designing/20260815-184832_sha3-preference-and-post-quantum-kumara.md).

Edges clamp so no read wraps; the edge map is a fresh **grayscale** view (`r==g==b`), alpha opaque. - **REDS #73 CLOSED `20260812` -- the spool (chunking storage) built** (reds-first crux, self-approved design round `active-designing/20260812-191206_gism-red73-spool-chunking-storage-exploration.md`). A booked red preempts the next durable journey, so before Season 2 Journey 7 (Fair-trade, AYRE) opens, the chunking horizon red #73 named is now a module. `mantra/spool.rye` winds a large artifact across **ordered resins** (each a valid bead-index over the shared `BeadStore`, fixed-size at 256 B for a legible store bound), the whole proving against a SHA3-256 content address unchanged by chunking, every tamper (flipped bead - corrupted resin index - truncated spool) refusing the whole reassembly. The very files red #73 refused now wind and unwind **byte-for-byte** -- `SECURITY.md` (2689 B -> 6 resins), `context/TWO_ROOMS.md` (6079 B -> 12 resins) -- and the honest **32 KB ceiling** is reachable in full (32768 B / 64 resins / 128 beads wound and unwound whole; over-ceiling refuses `ContentTooLarge`). No bound pretended: `max_resin_bytes` stayed structurally capped; the fix is a storage layer above it, with `beading.max_store_beads` raised 64 -> 256 **additively** so the ceiling is real (beading re-runs GREEN). Witness `tools/gism_spool_witness.rish` GREEN (real docs - ceiling - stable name - dedup dividend - three tamper refusals - on-disk size cross-check), TAME + width clean. **Then the naming layer LANDED `20260812` (spool r2):** `pond/apps/spool_cloth.rye` -- Tablecloth's sibling for large artifacts -- binds a human name to a whole spool, so a real front-door document too large for one resin is stored under a name and fetched back **byte-for-byte** (`SECURITY.md` 2689 B, `context/TWO_ROOMS.md` 6079 B), the same content under two names sharing every bead (full dedup: security-copy deposited 0, deduped 11), a `format spool-cloth-v1` manifest round-tripping name - address - length - resin count, and every refusal holding (tampered bead -> `DigestMismatch`, unknown/taken/empty name, bad manifest header). Composes the spool's public API only (spool re-runs GREEN); demo/real bytes only, no custody crosses. Witness `tools/gism_spool_cloth_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **Then the owner-signed large-artifact store LANDED `20260812` (spool r3) -- the payoff red #73 was reaching for:** `pond/apps/spool_keyed.rye` -- `tablecloth_keyed`'s sibling for large artifacts -- makes a settled Kumara identity vouch for which bytes: the owner presents the point's Deed (verified against the constellation) and signs the exact publication (point - whole length - name - **SHA-256 of the whole content**), and only then is the document wound across resins and named. Two real front-door documents (`SECURITY.md` 2689 B -> planet, `context/TWO_ROOMS.md` 6079 B -> star), each far too large for one resin, are now **owner-signed, wound across resins, fetched byte-for-byte, and resolved to whose they are** (`owner_of`/`place_of`). Every refusal holds -- a non-owner (ghost Deed -> `NotOwner`, no resin wound), a forged signature (`BadSignature`), a content-mismatched signature (the whole digest is bound in, so a tampered large document cannot ride a real signature), and a tampered bead after an honest publish (`DigestMismatch`). Composes `tablecloth_keyed`'s public signing helpers (`sign_publication`-`digest_of`-`verify_receipt`) over `spool_cloth` (both re-run GREEN); `spool_cloth` grew only a `pub index_of`. Demo keeper seeds only -- **no real key, no custody (gate #4 untouched)**. Witness `tools/gism_spool_keyed_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **Then the large-artifact portable receipt LANDED `20260812` (spool r4) -- the arc's finishing rung:** `pond/apps/spool_receipt.rye` -- the large-artifact sibling of TACT-J2's `publish_receipt`/`publish_receipt_bron` and GISM-J6r2's `provenance_receipt` (which travelled a receipt for a document small enough to fit one resin) -- carries a large owner-signed document's signed word *out of the spool* as a portable receipt. The receipt is content-length-independent by design (point - name - whole length - SHA-256 - signature -- the same signed word `spool_keyed` binds), so one `Publication` vouches for a document whether it lives in one resin or sixty; the crux over `provenance_receipt` is that the receipt is tied to bytes **reassembled from the resins**, not a whole-held copy. `SECURITY.md` (2689 B -> 6 resins) winds under the owner's signature, the detached receipt carries the **identical signature** the spool stored (proven byte-for-byte), travels as a `format publication-v1` Bron record (263 B) and returns byte-for-byte, still verifies offline (no store, no spool, no network), and the bytes read back out of the resins match the bound digest -- cross-checked against an independent `sha256sum` (`5d34cc8b...571c`, two tools one answer). Every tamper refuses (flipped reassembled byte - wrong keeper key - unknown Bron field). No fresh Yield red; composes `spool_keyed` + `publish_receipt` + `publish_receipt_bron` public API (all three re-run GREEN); no custody crosses. Witness `tools/gism_spool_receipt_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **The spool arc stands complete: chunking (r1) - naming (r2) - owner-signed (r3) - receipt-travels (r4) -- provenance for a document too large for one resin now stands on documents a keeper actually wrote and can hash by hand.**

- **Season 2 Journey 7 (Fair-trade, AYRE) OPENED `20260812` (AYRE-J7r1)** -- with the spool arc complete, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole 1,024-round road is Yield's second promise beyond *stands on real data*: **trades fairly** (self-approved exploration `active-designing/20260812-193727_ayre-journey7-fair-trade-exploration.md`; four rounds -- r1 satisfied - r2 signed - r3 travels - r4 tied-to-a-fair-trade -- under the seated **AYRE** waymark). **AYRE-J7r1 LANDED:** `pond/apps/fairtrade_cert.rye` fixes a fair-trade certificate's honest meaning as the join it is -- a `format fairtrade-cert-v1` certificate (one `require <criterion>` line per requirement) **evaluated against a cooperative `Record`** of held attestations, the same *every-required-name-is-held* join `tablecloth_brix.evaluate` proved for artifacts (BUHR-J7r3), carried from artifacts to fair-trade criteria. A record holding every required criterion yields a `Certified` naming exactly those criteria **in declared order**; a required criterion the record does not hold refuses the whole certification (`Unsatisfied`) rather than certify a gap -- a satisfied `Certified` exists only when every criterion was held, so its existence is the proof. Malformed refusals real (wrong header - bad tag - empty - two names on one line -> `BadCertificate`; overlong -> `NameTooLong`; past-bound -> `TooManyCriteria`). Pure declaration-satisfaction -- no signing/books yet (r2 signs each attestation mirroring `commerce_trade_signed`, r4 books fairly in Dimeroll mirroring `commerce_trade`); facts only, custody gate #3 untouched, demo attestations only. GREEN first pass; witness `tools/ayre_j7_fairtrade_cert_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **AYRE-J7r2 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/fairtrade_cert_signed.rye` binds each attestation to a settled Kumara identity -- the attesting party signs the exact fact (their point - the criterion name), and a satisfied certificate requires **every** required criterion's signature to verify against the attester's known keeper key, so a steward cannot certify a criterion no party vouched for. A `SignedRecord` carries name - attester point - signature per attestation; a `Keyring` maps point -> keeper key (the caller's statement of whose word to trust, as `commerce_trade_signed` passes the seller/buyer keys); `verify_held` refuses `Unsatisfied` (unheld) - `UnknownAttester` (unenrolled) - `BadAttestation` (unverifying) by exact reason; `evaluate_signed` reuses r1's parse + `Certified`. The honesty crux mirrors commerce's price-tamper -- a steward relabels a `price-floor` attestation to `living-wage` and reuses the signature, but verification is over `living-wage` which no one signed -> `BadAttestation`. Additive (composes `fairtrade_cert` + `kumara` public API; r1 re-runs GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, custody gate #3 untouched. Witness `tools/ayre_j7_fairtrade_cert_signed_witness.rish` GREEN (6 cases + r1 re-run), TAME + width clean. **AYRE-J7r3 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/fairtrade_cert_bron.rye` makes the satisfied, signed certificate travel -- the certified attestations render to a `format fairtrade-signed-v1` Bron record (one `attest <name> <point> <sig-hex>` line per criterion) and parse back byte-for-byte, **still satisfied**: a `PortableCert` owns its buffers, `still_satisfied` re-verifies each traveled attestation via r2's `verify_held`, and re-running `evaluate_signed` over the traveled bundle yields the identical `Certified`. A signature flipped after the crossing refuses `BadAttestation` **offline** (no store, no network); an unknown field (`UnknownField`, never dropped), a malformed line (`BadLine`), a wrong header (`BadFormat`), and bad hex (`BadHex`) each refuse; `render` refuses `NotCertified` rather than emit a lying line. Additive (composes `fairtrade_cert` + `fairtrade_cert_signed` + `kumara` public API; r2 re-runs GREEN); reads no network (serve transport = Comlink-served gate); demo keeper seeds only, custody gate #3 untouched. Witness `tools/ayre_j7_fairtrade_cert_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (3 cases + r2 re-run), TAME + width clean. **AYRE-J7r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 7 CLOSES:** `pond/apps/fairtrade_cert_trade.rye` ties the certificate to a fair-value trade -- `record_certified_trade` gates `commerce.record_trade` behind two proofs, **before any book is touched**: the trade's seller must be one of the cooperative's own attesters (`SellerNotCertified`, so the certificate cannot float free of the trade), and the certificate must be satisfied against the signed record (r2's `evaluate_signed`, refusing `Unsatisfied`/`UnknownAttester`/`BadAttestation`). Only then does the trade book fairly on both books at one agreed price (each nets to zero, the pair conserves across cash; `fair_trade_holds` folds it to one answer). Even a certified cooperative cannot book an unfair trade -- a self-trade (`SelfTrade`) and a zero price (`BadAmount`) refuse at commerce's own laws. Additive (composes `fairtrade_cert_signed` + `commerce_trade` public API, both re-run GREEN); facts only, holds nothing, settling reaches custody gate #3 (licensed counsel); demo seeds only. Witness `tools/ayre_j7_fairtrade_cert_trade_witness.rish` GREEN (5 cases + r2 + commerce re-runs), TAME + width clean. **AYRE Journey 7 (Fair-trade) stands complete end to end -- r1 satisfied - r2 signed - r3 travels - r4 tied-to-a-fair-trade.**

- **Season 2 Journey 8 (Cash-first) OPENED `20260812` (GISM-AYRE-J8r1)** -- with AYRE Journey 7 closed, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole road is Yield's last owning promise: **the accounting spine for Linengrow PBC / Siya Fund** (self-approved exploration `active-designing/20260812-195711_gism-ayre-journey8-cash-first-entity-books-exploration.md`; four rounds -- r1 segregated transfer - r2 signed - r3 period travels - r4 read-true). The seated entity-books design names the strand: **entity separation, never braided.** **GISM-AYRE-J8r1 LANDED:** `pond/apps/entity_books.rye` fixes the crux -- two named entities, each an `Entity` owning its name + bounded journal, folding its own trial balance (conserves) and balance sheet (balances, core-asserted) through `dimeroll_core`'s `fold_journal`/`fold_balance_sheet`. An inter-entity `transfer` books one fact on **both** journals (grantor debit-expense/credit-cash, grantee debit-cash/credit-revenue of one amount), so grantor-out == grantee-in by construction; each entity's books balance and the pair conserves across cash (`pair_conserves` isolates the transfer delta -- Siya -1500, Linengrow +1500, sum 0). Segregation proven **byte-identical** (a fact on Linengrow leaves Siya's fold unchanged). A self-transfer (`SameEntity`), a non-positive amount (`BadAmount`, no journal touched), and a full journal (`JournalFull`) each refuse. Additive (composes `dimeroll_core` public API, no new value model; its selftest re-runs GREEN); facts only, holds nothing, disbursement custody gate #3; demo entities only. Witness `tools/gism_ayre_j8_entity_books_witness.rish` GREEN (5 cases + dimeroll re-run), TAME + width clean. **GISM-AYRE-J8r2 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/entity_books_signed.rye` binds the transfer to two settled Kumara identities -- each entity's steward signs the exact fact (the two entity names length-prefixed - amount - memo), and `record_signed_transfer` books through r1's `transfer` only when both signatures verify, gating **before any journal is touched**: signed names must match the two entities' own (`NameMismatch`), then both must agree (`NotAgreed`). The honesty crux mirrors `commerce_trade_signed` -- change the amount after both signed and both signatures fall (`NotAgreed`, journal untouched); a forged signature refuses too. Additive (composes `entity_books` + `kumara` public API; r1 re-runs GREEN); demo steward seeds only, custody gate #3 untouched. Witness `tools/gism_ayre_j8_entity_books_signed_witness.rish` GREEN (4 cases + r1 re-run), TAME + width clean. **GISM-AYRE-J8r3 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/entity_books_period.rye` folds an entity's period to a sealed statement (name - close stamp - four trial-balance nets), renders it to a `format entity-period-v1` Bron record, and parses back preserving every field, the recovered period **still conserving and balancing** -- a `PortablePeriod` owns its buffers, refuses `Unbalanced` if its nets do not sum to zero (rather than travel as false books), else folds the balance sheet the core asserts balances (matching the sealed one field for field). An unknown field (`UnknownField`, never dropped), a missing net/header (`MissingField`), a wrong header (`BadFormat`), and a bad integer (`BadInt`) each refuse. Additive (composes r1's fold + `dimeroll_core` public API; r1 re-runs GREEN); reads no network (serve transport = Comlink-served gate); demo entities only, custody gate #3 untouched. Witness `tools/gism_ayre_j8_entity_books_period_witness.rish` GREEN (4 cases + r1 re-run), TAME + width clean. **GISM-AYRE-J8r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 8 CLOSES:** `pond/apps/entity_books_true.rye` carries the books onto a **real** Dimeroll journal fixture (`dimeroll/fixtures/journal.bron`, `@embedFile`-bound through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink) -- `read_journal` parses it with `dimeroll_core.parse_journal`, loads each fact into a rung-one `Entity`, folds (conservation + balance asserted on real bytes), and reports 2 entries - amount total 1200 - cash net 800. The crux: `tools/fixtures/entity_journal_truth.awk` measures the same file independently and the witness asserts the app's fold equals the awk reading field for field -- **two tools, one answer** -- so an entity's balance can never drift from facts a keeper adds up by hand. A malformed real journal refuses `UnknownAccount`. Additive (composes r1 `Entity` + `dimeroll_core` public API; r1 re-runs GREEN); reads no network; demo entities only, custody gate #3 untouched. Witness `tools/gism_ayre_j8_entity_books_true_witness.rish` GREEN (2 cases + app==awk crux + r1 re-run), TAME + width clean. **Season 2 Journey 8 (Cash-first) stands complete end to end -- r1 segregated transfer - r2 signed - r3 period travels - r4 read-true. Season 2 (Yield) agent-doable arc is complete across J5-J6-J7-J8.** **Next agent-doable crux (Lindy-first):** Season 3 (Commons, DAHL) -- Journey 9 (Skate social layer, a consent-first surface over Pond identities), a new domain worth a self-approved design read first.

- **Season 3 -- Commons OPENED `20260812` (DAHL-J9r1)** -- with Seasons 1 (TACT) and 2 (Yield) complete across every agent-doable rung, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole 1,024-round road is **Season 3 (Commons), Journey 9 (Skate)** under the seated **DAHL** waymark: the social layer over Pond identities, *a shared surface entered by consent, never by default* (self-approved design round `active-designing/20260812-201611_dahl-journey9-skate-consent-first-exploration.md`). Crux: **a link between two members exists only when both have requested it.** **DAHL-J9r1 LANDED:** `pond/apps/skate_circle.rye` carries Pond's own customs trichotomy (place - hold - refuse) to the social border, where the resting verdict is the strictest -- a `Circle` a keeper joins only by their own explicit act (no member by default), and a link that is **mutual-only**: one keeper's `request_link` is a standing offer that **holds**; the connection **places** (links) only when the other has also asked, so a one-sided request holds forever and a steward cannot manufacture a connection from one side. A link is not stored but **derived** as the presence of two opposing requests, so the mutual-only law cannot be bypassed by writing a link directly; `visible` follows `linked` exactly (invariant-asserted), so presence is never default -- a joined keeper who linked no one sees no one and is seen by no one. Every refusal holds: a link touching a non-member (`NotMember`), a self-link (`SelfLink`), a taken seat (`AlreadyMember`), a repeated offer (`AlreadyRequested`), a full circle (`CircleFull`). Pure state machine -- no signing yet (r2 binds each act to a settled Kumara identity mirroring `commerce_trade_signed`; r3 travels as Bron; r4 reads a real fixture true). Facts only, custody-first: no network, no key, no real identity (a circle served over the wire reaches the serve gate -- Keaton's hand); demo keeper points only. Witness `tools/dahl_j9_skate_circle_witness.rish` GREEN (2 welcomes + 4 refusals), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J9r2 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/skate_circle_signed.rye` binds each consent act to the keeper's settled Kumara identity -- a join is signed by the joining point, a link-request by the requesting point, over the exact facts (a `tag` so a join can never be replayed as a request, the `circle_id` so consent cannot cross circles, the points). A `Keyring` maps point -> keeper key (the caller's statement of whose word to trust, as `commerce_trade_signed` passes seller/buyer keys); `join_signed`/`request_signed` verify the acting keeper's **own** signature before touching r1's circle, refusing `UnknownKeeper` (unenrolled) or `BadConsent` (unverifying). The honesty crux mirrors commerce's price-tamper -- relabel a request's target after signing and reuse the signature, it falls `BadConsent` (no offer recorded); a join signed by the wrong keeper (`BadConsent`, no seat taken), an unenrolled keeper (`UnknownKeeper`), and a signature presented to another circle (`BadConsent`) each refuse. Additive (composes `skate_circle` + `kumara` public API; r1 re-runs GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, no real key, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j9_skate_circle_signed_witness.rish` GREEN (1 welcome + 4 refusals + r1 re-run), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J9r3 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/skate_circle_bron.rye` makes the signed circle travel -- a `SignedCircle` keeps, beside r1's live circle, the signature that authorized each act (a join sig per member, a request sig per link), renders to a `format skate-circle-v1` Bron record and parses back **byte-for-byte**, still consent-honest **offline**: `still_consented` re-verifies every carried signature against the recipient's keyring through r2's `verify_join`/`verify_request` (exposed additively -- r2 re-runs GREEN), and `rebuild` replays the acts through r2's verified paths into an equal live circle whose `linked`/`visible` verdicts match. A signature flipped after the crossing refuses `BadConsent` offline (no store, no network, no live circle); an unknown field (`UnknownField`, never dropped), a wrong header (`BadFormat`), a missing circle id (`MissingCircle`), and malformed hex (`BadHex`) each refuse. Additive (composes r1 + r2 public API; both re-run GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j9_skate_circle_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (1 welcome + 5 refusals + r1/r2 re-runs), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J9r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 9 CLOSES:** `pond/apps/skate_circle_true.rye` carries the reader onto a **real** circle record -- `skate/fixtures/circle.bron`, a genuine `format skate-circle-v1` record with real Ed25519 signatures (3 members - 1 mutual link - 1 one-sided hold), `@embedFile`-bound through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink. Two readings must agree: the app parses through r3 and reports member count - link count - **mutual-link count** (an unordered pair both directed requests appear -- a one-sided hold not counted), and the witness cross-checks that triple against `tools/fixtures/skate_circle_truth.awk`'s reading of the same file -- **two tools, one answer** (`3 3 1`). And the record is genuinely consent-honest -- `still_consented` re-verifies every carried signature against the demo keyring, and `rebuild` recovers the mutual link (`linked(ivy,wren)`) and the one-sided hold (`verdict(flax,ivy)==hold`, not visible) exactly -- so the fixture is a real signed circle, not a hollow shape. A tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Additive (composes r1-r2-r3 public API, all re-run GREEN); reads no network; demo keeper seeds only. Witness `tools/dahl_j9_skate_circle_true_witness.rish` GREEN (2 welcomes + 1 refusal + app==awk crux + r1/r3 re-runs), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL Journey 9 (Skate) stands complete end to end -- r1 consent - r2 signed - r3 travels - r4 read-true.** The one remaining Skate horizon rung -- **Comlink-served** (a circle shared over the wire) -- reaches the serve custody gate (Keaton's hand). **Next agent-doable crux (Lindy-first):** Season 3 Journey 10 (Membership) -- the one surface that needs quorum (names, groups) proven at more than two keepers, extending `settlement/names.rye`; a fresh self-approved design read first.

- **Season 3 Journey 10 (Membership) OPENED `20260812` (DAHL-J10r1)** -- with Journey 9 closed, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole road is the one surface `settlement/names.rye` names in its own header as *the surface that genuinely needs consensus*, proven now at **more than two keepers** (self-approved design round `active-designing/20260812-204233_dahl-journey10-membership-quorum-exploration.md`). J9 proved *exactly-two* mutual consent; J10's durable new law is *threshold-of-n* quorum. Crux: **a candidate is seated only when a quorum of distinct members votes.** **DAHL-J10r1 LANDED:** `pond/apps/skate_group.rye` -- a `Group` opens with a founder roster and a fixed quorum `t` (`2 <= t <= roster` -- genuine consensus, never one hand, never a unanimity a lone hold-out could veto); a member votes for a candidate; the candidate is admitted only when `t` **distinct** members have voted. The honesty is distinctness with threshold -- one keeper voting twice is counted once (`AlreadyVoted`), so a lone steward can never manufacture a quorum; admission is **derived** from the distinct-vote count reaching `t` (there is no direct add-member), and `is_member` follows the verdict exactly (invariant-asserted). Proven past two keepers: a quorum of three admits only when three distinct members vote (two hold). Every refusal holds -- a non-member vote (`NotMember`), re-admitting a seated keeper (`AlreadyMember`), a lone quorum (`BadThreshold`), an unreachable quorum (`TooFewFounders`), a doubled founder (`DuplicateFounder`), a full roster refusing the quorum-reaching vote before it overflows (`GroupFull`), a full vote book (`VotesFull`). Pure state machine -- no signing yet (r2 signs each vote with the member's settled Kumara identity mirroring `skate_circle_signed`; r3 travels as Bron; r4 reads a real fixture true). Facts only, custody-first: no network, no key, no real identity (a group served over the wire reaches the serve gate -- Keaton's hand); demo keeper points only. Witness `tools/dahl_j10_skate_group_witness.rish` GREEN (2 welcomes + 4 refusals), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J10r2 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/skate_group_signed.rye` binds each vote to the voting member's settled Kumara identity -- a vote is signed by the very member it speaks for over the exact facts (a `tag`, the `group_id` so a vote cannot cross groups, the voter, the candidate); a `Keyring` maps point -> keeper key (the caller's statement of whose word to trust, as `skate_circle_signed` passes keys), and `vote_signed` verifies the voter's **own** signature before touching r1's group, refusing `UnknownKeeper` (unenrolled -- even for a real member) or `BadConsent` (unverifying). The honesty crux mirrors the circle's relabel -- change the candidate after signing and reuse the signature, it falls `BadConsent` (no vote recorded); a forged vote by the wrong keeper (`BadConsent`, tally unmoved), a cross-group signature (`BadConsent`) each refuse. And the lone-steward guard carries through r1 unchanged -- a member's valid signature lets them vote once, the repeat refusing `AlreadyVoted` with the tally held at one, so signing still cannot fake a quorum from one hand. Founders are the group's seeded bootstrap; every admission after takes signed distinct votes. Additive (composes `skate_group` + `kumara` public API; r1 re-runs GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, no real key, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j10_skate_group_signed_witness.rish` GREEN (1 welcome + 5 refusals + r1 re-run), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J10r3 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/skate_group_bron.rye` makes the signed group travel -- a `SignedGroup` keeps, beside r1's live group, its founder roster - quorum - the signature that authorized each vote, renders to a `format skate-group-v1` Bron record (a `group <id> <quorum>` line - one `founder` line each - one `vote <voter> <candidate> <sig-hex>` line each) and parses back **byte-for-byte**, still quorum-honest **offline**: `still_quorum_honest` re-verifies every carried vote signature against the recipient's keyring through r2's `verify_vote`, and `rebuild` replays the founders and votes through r1's `open` and r2's `vote_signed` into an equal live group whose admission matches (Bram still seated, verdict `.place`). A vote signature flipped after the crossing refuses `BadConsent` offline (no store, no network, no live group); an unknown field (`UnknownField`, never dropped), a wrong header (`BadFormat`), a missing group line (`MissingGroup`), and malformed hex (`BadHex`) each refuse. Additive (composes r1 + r2 public API; both re-run GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j10_skate_group_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (1 welcome + 5 refusals + r1/r2 re-runs), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J10r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 10 CLOSES:** `pond/apps/skate_group_true.rye` carries the reader onto a **real** group record -- `skate/fixtures/group.bron`, a genuine `format skate-group-v1` record with real Ed25519 vote signatures (3 founders at quorum 2 - 2 votes admit Bram - 1 one-sided vote for Cate holds), `@embedFile`-bound through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink, produced reproducibly by an in-tree generator (`pond/apps/skate_group_fixture_gen.rye`, demo seeds only). Two readings must agree: the app parses through r3 and reports founder count - vote count - **admitted-by-quorum count** (distinct candidates whose distinct-vote tally reaches the quorum -- a candidate short of quorum not counted), and the witness cross-checks that triple against `tools/fixtures/skate_group_truth.awk`'s reading of the same file -- **two tools, one answer** (`3 3 1`). And the record is genuinely quorum-honest -- `still_quorum_honest` re-verifies every carried vote signature against the demo keyring, and `rebuild` recovers Bram's admission (`.place`) and Cate's hold (`.hold`, not seated) exactly, so the fixture is a real signed group, not a hollow shape. A tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Additive (composes r1-r2-r3 public API, all re-run GREEN); reads no network; demo keeper seeds only. Witness `tools/dahl_j10_skate_group_true_witness.rish` GREEN (2 welcomes + 1 refusal + app==awk crux + r1/r3 re-runs), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL Journey 10 (Membership) stands complete end to end -- r1 quorum - r2 signed - r3 travels - r4 read-true.** The one remaining Membership horizon rung -- **Comlink-served** (a group shared over the wire) -- reaches the serve custody gate (Keaton's hand). **Next agent-doable crux (Lindy-first):** Season 3 Journey 11 (Council skies) -- loadable topologies a community loads like a game (odd-quorum fractal `3-9-15-27`, modality roles), a new domain worth a fresh self-approved design read first.

- **Season 3 Journey 11 (Council skies) OPENED `20260812` (DAHL-J11r1)** -- with Journey 10 closed, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole road is the loadable-topologies brief's load-bearing move: a community does not merely pick a threshold (J10), it **loads a whole shape** -- a *sky* -- the way a console loads a game, and the sky's own odd fractal *gives* the council its tie-free quorum and its roles (self-approved design round `active-designing/20260812-210603_dahl-journey11-council-skies-exploration.md`). Crux: **an odd sky gives a tie-free quorum and a complete triad.** **DAHL-J11r1 LANDED:** `pond/apps/council_sky.rye` composes `comlink/topology.rye`'s `Sky` -- a `Council` loads a sky and seats a body at one of the sky's own fractal levels (`galaxies - stars - planets - prosperity`). The **council sky's** four counts are **3 - 9 - 15 - 27**, every one odd; the quorum is the strict majority of the odd body (`size/2+1`), and because the body is odd, `2-quorum > size` (invariant-asserted) -- yes and no can never both reach the quorum, so a fully-voted proposal is **always decided, never a tie**. The civic **`compass_sky` is not a council sky**: its `prosperity` is 60 -- even -- so seating its d60 refuses `EvenCouncil` (its odd star level 5 still seats, quorum 3). Each seat wears a **modality role** -- `initiator - sustainer - adapter` by `seat mod 3` -- a pure function of position, so any triad is complete and can never drift from the seated body; the outcome is **derived** from the tally, never written. Refusals hold -- a size the sky does not name (`NotAFractalLevel`), an even level (`EvenCouncil`), a non-seat vote (`NotSeated`), a doubled ballot (`AlreadyVoted`, tally unmoved). Pure state machine -- no signing yet (r2 signs each vote with the member's Kumara identity mirroring `skate_group_signed`; r3 travels as Bron; r4 reads a real fixture true). Facts only, custody-first: no network, no key, no real identity (a council served over the wire reaches the serve gate -- Keaton's hand); demo seats only. A compound `assert(a and b)` caught by `tame_style_check` and split. Witness `tools/dahl_j11_council_sky_witness.rish` GREEN (6 cases), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J11r2 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/council_sky_signed.rye` binds each ballot to the voting seat's settled Kumara identity -- a ballot is signed by the very seat it speaks for over the exact facts (a `tag`, the `council_id` so a ballot cannot cross councils, the council **size** so it cannot cross a differently-shaped body, the seat, the proposal, the yes/no); a `Keyring` maps a seat to its keeper key; `vote_signed` verifies the seat's own signature before touching r1's council, refusing `UnknownKeeper` (unenrolled) or `BadConsent` (unverifying). The honesty crux mirrors the group's relabel -- flip a ballot's yes to no after signing and reuse the signature, it falls `BadConsent`; a forged ballot (wrong keeper), a cross-council signature each refuse. The odd-quorum lone-hand guard carries through r1 (`AlreadyVoted`, tally held), so signing still cannot fake a majority. Additive (composes r1 + `kumara`; r1 re-runs GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, no real key, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j11_council_sky_signed_witness.rish` GREEN (2 welcomes + 4 refusals + r1 re-run), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J11r3 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/council_sky_bron.rye` renders a signed council to a `format council-sky-v1` Bron record (a `council` line carrying the sky's galaxy/star/planet counts + id + seated size, one `ballot` line per signed vote) and parses back byte-for-byte. Still tie-free and quorum-honest offline -- `still_quorum_honest` re-verifies each carried ballot via r2's `verify_ballot`; `rebuild` reconstructs the sky and re-seats through r1's `load`, **inheriting `EvenCouncil` so a record naming the compass sky's even d60 (12-5-12-60) refuses on rebuild** (the tie-free law travels with the record, distinct from `skate_group_bron`). Unknown/missing field - bad header - bad hex - non-0/1 yea each refuse. Additive (composes r1 + r2; both re-run GREEN); demo keeper seeds only, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j11_council_sky_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (1 welcome + 6 refusals/re-runs), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J11r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 11 CLOSES:** `pond/apps/council_sky_true.rye` `@embedFile`-binds `skate/fixtures/council.bron` (a genuine `format council-sky-v1` record with real Ed25519 ballot signatures -- the council sky's triad, size 3, quorum 2; two yes-ballots decide proposal A, one yes-ballot holds proposal B open; produced reproducibly by `pond/apps/council_sky_fixture_gen.rye`, demo seeds only) through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink, parses through r3, and reports the seated **size**, **ballot count**, and **decided-proposal count**. The crux: `tools/fixtures/council_sky_truth.awk` reads the size and majority quorum, dedups ballots, and counts decided proposals independently, and the witness asserts the app's triple equals awk's -- two tools, one answer (`3 3 1`). The record is genuinely tie-free and quorum-honest -- `still_quorum_honest` re-verifies every ballot, `rebuild` recovers proposal A's passage and proposal B's open hold exactly, `2-quorum > size` holding on the real record. A tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Additive (composes r1-r2-r3; all re-run GREEN); reads no network; demo keeper seeds only. Witness `tools/dahl_j11_council_sky_true_witness.rish` GREEN (2 welcomes + 1 refusal + app==awk crux + r1/r3 re-runs), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL Journey 11 (Council skies) stands complete end to end -- r1 odd quorum - r2 signed - r3 travels - r4 read-true.** The one remaining Council horizon rung -- **Comlink-served** (a council shared over the wire) -- reaches the serve custody gate (Keaton's hand). **Next agent-doable crux (Lindy-first):** Season 3 Journey 12 (Data-dignity) -- succession, consent, the keeper's rights, custody-first; a new domain worth a fresh self-approved design read first.

- **Season 3 Journey 12 (Data-dignity) OPENED `20260812` (DAHL-J12r1)** -- with Journey 11 closed, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole road is Commons' last and most tender: **what becomes of a keeper's data when the keeper is gone** (self-approved design round `active-designing/20260812-212607_dahl-journey12-data-dignity-exploration.md`). Journeys 9-11 built how *living* keepers meet; Journey 12 carries consent, and the keeper's own word, past a single lifetime. Crux: **succession honors the keeper's declared word, and rests what the keeper chose to rest.** **DAHL-J12r1 LANDED:** `pond/apps/data_dignity.rye` -- an `Estate` opens naming a keeper and a **distinct** heir (`SelfHeir` refused); the keeper wills each holding **bequeathed** (passes to the heir) or **rested** (the keeper's dignity choice -- extinguished at succession, inherited by no one, returned to no hand), **seals** the directive (the pre-declared word, fixed while present), and the heir **accepts**. Succession fires only on **two proofs, never one** -- the sealed directive (`NotSealed`) *and* the heir's acceptance (`NotAccepted`) -- so neither the departing keeper's word alone nor the receiving heir's alone moves custody (an heir is never conscripted; a steward cannot manufacture a will): the settlement ledger's escape law (both consent, the old sponsor keeps no veto) carried across the one boundary it had not crossed, **the keeper's absence**. Who holds an item is **derived**, never written -- before succession every willed item is the keeper's; after, a bequeathed item is the heir's and a rested item is `rested` (no one's), an unwilled item `unknown`. The rested set's extinction is the promise made checkable -- `heir_held_count == bequeathed_count` (asserted in `succeed`), and no rested item is ever the heir's -- so the one preference a departed person can no longer defend is the one the rule guards hardest. Refusals hold -- a self-heir (`SelfHeir`), a re-willed item (`AlreadyWilled`), a word after seal (`Sealed`, the keeper's word is one and fixed), a replayed succession (`AlreadySucceeded`, custody passes once). Pure state machine -- no signing yet (r2 signs the keeper's seal and the heir's acceptance with settled Kumara identities, mirroring `skate_group_signed`; r3 travels as Bron; r4 reads a real fixture true). Facts only, custody-first: no network, no funds, no key, no real seed (the maintainer's own succession over a real Kumara instance is the maintainer's hand -- gate #4-adjacent); demo keeper and heir points only. Witness `tools/dahl_j12_data_dignity_witness.rish` GREEN (5 selftests), TAME bans + width clean. **DAHL-J12r2 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/data_dignity_signed.rye` binds **each of the two proofs** to a settled Kumara identity -- the keeper's seal to the keeper's key, the heir's acceptance to the heir's key. The keeper's directive signs a tag - estate id - keeper - heir - a **digest of the will's own marks** (SHA-256 over every willed item + disposition, in order); the honesty crux is that `seal_signed` recomputes the digest from the estate's **current** marks, so a bequeathed->rested tamper after the keeper signed falls `BadConsent` -- a steward cannot rewrite a departed keeper's will. The heir's acceptance signs tag - estate id - the two points, so it cannot cross estates. Refusals hold -- tampered will - forged directive - forged acceptance - unenrolled party (`UnknownKeeper`) - cross-estate signature. Succession stays r1's unchanged two-proof gate. Additive -- r1 grew only two pub readers (`item_at`, `disposition_at`); r1 re-runs GREEN. Two build reds caught on metal (a param shadowing a demo const; SHA-256 streaming method is `final`) fixed same-round. Demo seeds only; gate #4-adjacent untouched. Witness `tools/dahl_j12_data_dignity_signed_witness.rish` GREEN (5 + r1 re-run), TAME + width clean. **DAHL-J12r3 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/data_dignity_bron.rye` renders a signed estate to a `format data-dignity-v1` Bron record (`estate <id> <keeper> <heir>` - one `hold <item> <disposition>` per holding - `directive`/`acceptance` sig-hex) and parses back **byte-for-byte** into an owning `PortableEstate`. Still dignity-honest **offline** -- `still_dignity_honest` re-verifies both proofs, `rebuild` replays through r2's signed gates and succeeds, the rested set still extinguished (`heir_held_count == bequeathed_count`). The crux: a `hold` mark flipped rested->bequeathed after the crossing shifts the will digest -> the directive falls `BadConsent`, so the marks travel **under** the keeper's signature; a flipped directive refuses both check and rebuild. Unknown field - bad header - missing estate - missing proof - bad disposition - bad hex each refuse. Additive -- r1/r2 re-run GREEN; GREEN first pass. Demo seeds only, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j12_data_dignity_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (9 + r1/r2 re-runs), TAME + width clean. **DAHL-J12r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 12 CLOSES:** `pond/apps/data_dignity_true.rye` `@embedFile`-binds `skate/fixtures/estate.bron` (a genuine `format data-dignity-v1` record with real Ed25519 directive + acceptance signatures -- 2 bequeathed, 1 rested; produced reproducibly by `pond/apps/data_dignity_fixture_gen.rye`) through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink, parses through r3, and reports **bequeathed - rested - heir-held**, the last computed by *actually running* the signed succession (`rebuild` -> seal/accept/succeed) and resolving each item's holder. The crux: `tools/fixtures/data_dignity_truth.awk` reads the marks structurally and the witness asserts app==awk (`2 1 2`) -- two genuinely different computations agreeing proves the succession yields exactly the bequeathed set, the rested holding resting. `still_dignity_honest` re-verifies both real proofs; a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. One build red (mixed parse+rebuild error sets -> merged return type) fixed same-round. Additive -- r1/r3 re-run GREEN; demo seeds only, no network. Witness `tools/dahl_j12_data_dignity_true_witness.rish` GREEN (3 + app==awk + r1/r3 re-runs), TAME + width clean. **DAHL Journey 12 (Data-dignity) stands complete end to end -- r1 two-proof rule - r2 signed - r3 travels - r4 read-true. Season 3 (Commons) agent-doable arc COMPLETE across J9-J10-J11-J12.** The Comlink-served rung is the serve custody gate. **Next agent-doable crux:** Season 4 (Seal, CION) -- Journey 13 (Survey, VOLS), a fresh self-approved design read first, Lindy-first.

- **Season 4 -- Seal (CION meta-season) OPENED `20260812` (VOLS-J13r1)** -- with Season 3 (Commons) closed end to end, the next Lindy-first crux on the whole 1,024-round road is **Season 4 (Seal), Journey 13 (Survey, VOLS)**: name every living count-up ID, each site and each gap, before a single relabel (self-approved design `active-designing/20260812-215153_vols-journey13-survey-complete-census-exploration.md`). CION's opening lap swept seven modules *by hand* into `cion_module_labeling_witness`'s scan list -- proving the pattern (a bare `lap N` is drift; `lap 4b`/`OA-L3`/`sub-lap` are permitted codes) yet not *completeness*: the guard scans only what a human remembered, exactly how a human -- not a witness -- caught red #65. **VOLS-J13r1 LANDED:** a census **complete by construction**. `tools/fixtures/vols_survey_scan.sh` **derives** its input set from the tree with `find` (never hand-lists), excludes dated testimony structurally (session-logs - counsel - archive - dated specs keep the ordinals history earned) and the `seed/` projection (a derived copy, named at its source), and **reports** every living surface naming a capability by a bare `lap N` -- a survey reports, it does not gate (the GRAD seal, not VOLS, fails on drift). `tools/gen/season/vols_survey_witness.rish` GREEN proves it on a controlled fixture tree (a living README counted - a dated log excluded - `lap 4b`/`sub-lap 2` uncounted) and cross-checks the hit count against an independent grep -r traversal (`tools/fixtures/vols_survey_crosscount.sh`) -- two tools, one answer. **Live source-tree census: 2690 surfaces - 223 sites - 1146 hits** -- the real map LOWE will relabel against (223 sites vs 7 swept modules). Reads the tree, writes a census; touches no key, funds, network, or dated testimony; opens no relabel. **VOLS-J13r2 LANDED `20260812`:** each site classified by **kind** (prose relabel-target vs kept witness handle) and **guard** (watched vs gap). `tools/fixtures/vols_guardlist_extract.sh` derives the 29 guarded paths from the guard witness in force; `tools/fixtures/vols_classify_scan.sh` classifies each site against it. Crux invariant: the CION guard passing GREEN means every guarded file is clean, so **no site sits in a guarded file -- `guarded_sites=0` by construction**, proven (and the classifier bites a planted drifted guarded file, `verdict=drift`). **Live: 223 sites = 144 kept witness handles + 79 prose gaps** (the real LOWE relabel worklist), `guarded_sites=0`. Witness `tools/gen/season/vols_classify_witness.rish` GREEN. **VOLS-J13r3 LANDED `20260812`:** the census travels as a durable `format cion-survey-v1` Bron ledger. `tools/fixtures/vols_survey_render.sh` emits the record (header - stamp - totals - one `site <path> <kind> <guard>` line each, sorted); `tools/fixtures/vols_survey_parse.sh` reads the totals back, counts the site lines so the record proves itself, and refuses a bad header (`verdict=badformat`). Round-trip proven three ways: parse recovers what render wrote, the render is **idempotent** (identical bytes twice), a tampered header refuses. Witness `tools/gen/season/vols_survey_bron_witness.rish` GREEN. **VOLS-J13r4 LANDED `20260812` -- Journey 13 CLOSES:** the ledger **reads true** against an independent measure and is seated as a living pin. `tools/fixtures/vols_survey_truth.sh` computes the totals a second way (`grep -rl` traversal, basename classification -- genuinely different from the classifier's find-loop); `tools/fixtures/vols_survey_readtrue.sh` emits `readtrue=agree` only when sites/handles/prose_gaps each match, no number hardcoded. Both agree on the live tree: **223 sites = 144 handles + 79 prose gaps**, `guarded_sites=0`. Seated living pin `work-in-progress/vols-survey-ledger.bron` (223 classified site lines) proves itself self-consistent. Witness `tools/gen/season/vols_survey_true_witness.rish` GREEN. **VOLS Journey 13 (Survey) stands complete -- r1 derived census - r2 classified - r3 travels - r4 read-true + seated pin; the map LOWE relabels against is on metal.** **Next agent-doable crux:** Season 4 Journey 14 (Molt, LOWE) -- grow the CION guard to cover the 79 prose gaps and relabel them to semantic label + stamp; the survey names them all, so the molt is a filled worklist.

A **middot-byte trap** (a `.`-for-`-` regex orphaned a UTF-8 byte on a README line) was caught, reverted with `git checkout`, redone byte-exact, and every edited file verified valid UTF-8 by `iconv`. Comment+README only; proven safe on metal (`mandi_listing_settle`, `mandi_lap1`, `dimeroll_lap4`, `gism_ayre_j8` all GREEN). 8 surfaces added to the guard; CION guard GREEN; `guarded_sites` 28 -> 36 of 79; width + tame clean. **Next:** LOWE-J14r4b -- glow/nock (phased-plan prose, check the print-string witness first) + the living `tools/*.rish` prose, then re-run VOLS to close Journey 14. **LOWE-J14r4b LANDED `20260812`:** **glow/nock** (12 sites), the last module cluster -- its phased-plan spine named by phase (`Lap 1 -- the primitive core` -> `The primitive core`, macro layer, edit primitive) across `nock_core.rye`, `nock_core_witness.rye`, `README.md`. Used the Edit tool (em-dashes made byte-regex fragile). Proven on metal -- `nock_core_witness` GREEN; valid UTF-8, residual 0. 3 surfaces added; CION guard GREEN; `guarded_sites` 36 -> 39 of 79 (**all six module clusters done**). **Next:** LOWE-J14r4c -- the living `tools/*.rish` prose + mantra + the two README front doors + the `.glow` (~40 surfaces), then re-run VOLS so `guarded_sites` reaches 79, closing Journey 14. **LOWE-J14r4c LANDED `20260812`:** **mantra** (5 surfaces -- the last module weave), snapshot/recall prose named by capability (`NS-L3 wire lap 1` -> `NS-L3 wire`, `I2 snapshot export lap 3/2` -> `I2 snapshot export`, `Lap 1 reuses`/`Lap 2 adds` -> `Snapshot frames reuse`/`Horizon bundles add`) across README + four `.rye`, their hosted GREEN prints relabeled off the ordinal after confirming no witness asserts `contains` on them. Valid UTF-8, residual 0. 5 surfaces added; CION guard GREEN; `guarded_sites` 39 -> 44 of 79. **Next:** LOWE-J14r4d -- the ~35 remaining `tools/*.rish` witness-prose + 2 README front doors + `tools/gen/ember/README.md` + the `.glow` (~500 hits, mostly witnesses referencing the kept lap-handles -- keep each handle, name each capability), then re-run VOLS so `guarded_sites` reaches 79, closing Journey 14. **LOWE-J14r4d-i LANDED `20260812`:** the **witness doc-comment header cluster** -- thirteen `tools/*.rish` whose bare `lap N` sat only in a top `#` comment, the safest sub-cluster (zero behavior coupling: no `say`/`assert` string, no cross-witness `contains` dependency, so the relabel cannot break a driver): the four rishi stdlib witnesses (`filter_chain`/`fold`/`lines_bounded`/`quote_safe`), the three Acme DX conformance witnesses (`onboarding_path`/`interfaces`/`operations`, named by contract + stamp `20260811`), `granary_exchange`, the two mantra hosted labs (`snapshot_replay`/`snapshot_horizon`), `neth_sim_witness`, `nock_core_witness`, `wov_exit_honesty` -- each relabeled to capability + its own git-author stamp, the filename kept as the stable handle. - **Constel test-network naming law SEATED `20260813`** (`active-designing/20260813-022222_constel-test-network-naming-law.md`, self-approved -- the double-seat expansion reserved this round). A **constel** is a fake, self-invented constellation for a sandbox/testnet/localhost Comlink p2p network run **inside the jailed pier** (as Urbit's fake-galaxy dev nets stood to Arvo). One load-bearing promise, structural: **every constel name carries a digit**, so it can never parse as a real `@p` (letters + hyphens only, no digit in the 256-syllable table) -- the certainty placeholder-ship-names earns from segment length, the shape `xykj61`/`xnkg30` already wear. Seated the law doc, a first-draws fixture (`xnqvel34 - xzkerith58 - xwmur-dol12 - maqwintel06 - sunveknar90`), `tools/fixtures/constel_naming_scan.sh`, and `tools/constel_naming_witness.rish` GREEN (5 seated names safe; planted `@p`-shaped `sarlev` bites `verdict=unsafe`). Siloed, dev-only -- never a seed artifact, never a real address, never one of the maintainer's real points; reaches no gate. Aesthetic revisable; the digit is the only guarded invariant. When the real Comlink dev-net harness is built it draws from this law.

- **Constel dev-net harness r1 LANDED `20260813`** (`active-designing/20260813-022908_constel-devnet-harness-exploration.md`, self-approved) -- the harness the naming law named its own next rung. With the fixed 1,024-round road complete and no red open, the next Lindy-first crux-first move in Season D is the dev-net every future Comlink/settlement/Mycelium test runs against inside the jail. `pond/apps/constel_net.rye` fixes the crux: **a fake dev-net can only be born from a provably-non-`@p` name (a digit anywhere), and under that name it runs the real settlement protocol from genesis** -- the name carries the quarantine, not a flag. `spin_up` refuses `UnsafeName` **before** any genesis opens (structural: no dev-net exists un-quarantined); `name_safe` mirrors `constel_naming_scan.sh`'s digit-check in Rye, so the harness draws from the seated law; then a real `settlement.open` galaxy opens from demo seeds and its Deed verifies (composed exactly as `ship_pilot_identity`). Selftest GREEN (5 cases): safe-name genesis - all 5 seated draws safe - `@p`-shaped `sarlev` refuses `UnsafeName` no genesis - non-galaxy number refuses `NotGenesis` behind a safe name - empty/overlong refuse before genesis. Witness `tools/constel_net_witness.rish` GREEN, re-runs the naming law at its door; TAME + width clean; no bare-ordinal prose. Demo seeds only -- no key, no network, no custody (gate #4 untouched; a served constel reaches the serve gate). **Constel dev-net harness r2 LANDED `20260813`:** `pond/apps/constel_net_topology.rye` grows the fake net downward -- a **star beneath the galaxy, a planet beneath the star**, each seated by `settlement.mint` under its rightful d12-d60 topology sponsor (galaxy 3 - star 27 - planet 267, the same branch `settlement/names.rye` seats on real ground), so the **whole fractal address space settles inside the quarantined net**, every point crowned by the one law-safe name. Sponsorship is enforced, not claimed: a planet minted directly under the galaxy refuses `WrongSponsor` (it must climb through its real star), and a forged spawn authority refuses `BadAuthority` -- the real settlement protocol runs behind the name gate, so the fractal the tree settles on real ground settles identically on the dev bench. Composes r1's `spin_up` public API (r1 re-runs GREEN at r2's door); the galaxy keeper sows the star, the star keeper sows the planet (the same authority chain the ledger enforces). Selftest GREEN (3 cases: full branch settles + verifies - wrong-sponsor bites - forged-authority bites). Witness `tools/constel_net_topology_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/network/custody. **Constel dev-net harness r3 LANDED `20260813`:** `pond/apps/constel_net_bron.rye` makes the net **travel as text** -- `from_settled` builds a `PortableConstel` (owned name buffer + roster of point numbers) from r2's settled branch; `render` emits a `format constel-net-v1` record (a `net <name>` line + one `point <number>` line each, galaxy - star - planet in settle order); `parse` reads it back **byte-for-byte** (re-rendering the parsed record reproduces identical bytes -- a fixed point). The distinctive r3 crux: **the quarantine travels with the bytes** -- `parse` re-checks the naming law, so a record whose net name carries no digit refuses `UnsafeName` (a roster can never be read under a name that could parse as a real @p), exactly as `spin_up` refuses one at genesis. Purpose-built bounded flat-Bron reader: unknown field -> `UnknownField` (never dropped) - wrong header -> `BadFormat` - missing net line -> `MissingNet` - non-numeric point -> `BadInt` each refuse. Composes r1 (`name_safe`) + r2 (`SettledNet`) public API; the witness re-runs r2 GREEN at r3's door. Selftest GREEN (6 cases: round-trip recovers name+roster - unsafe-name-travels bites - unknown/header/missing-net/bad-int refuse). Witness `tools/constel_net_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/network/custody. **Constel dev-net harness r4 LANDED `20260813` -- the harness's agent-doable arc CLOSES:** `pond/apps/constel_net_true.rye` carries the reader onto a **real on-disk constel record** -- `tools/fixtures/constel_net.bron`, produced reproducibly by `pond/apps/constel_net_fixture_gen.rye` (the branch galaxy 3 - star 27 - planet 267 under `xnqvel34`), `@embedFile`-bound through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink. Two readings must agree: the app parses through r3 and reports **roster size - galaxy - planet**, and the witness cross-checks that triple against `tools/fixtures/constel_net_truth.awk` -- **two tools, one answer (`3 3 267`)**. The record is a genuine, law-safe harness net: re-rendering the parsed roster reproduces the embedded bytes exactly (the fixture is what the harness emits, not a hand-mock), and the recovered name still carries a digit (the quarantine holds on the real bytes); a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Composes r3 public API (r3 re-runs GREEN at r4's door). Selftest GREEN (3 cases + app==awk crux), TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/network/custody. **The Constel dev-net harness stands complete end to end -- r1 named genesis - r2 the fractal settles - r3 the net travels - r4 reads true.** The one remaining harness horizon rung -- **Comlink-served** (a constel shared over the wire) -- reaches the serve custody gate (Keaton's hand). **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey worth a self-approved design read -- the dev-net now stands as a bench, so the natural next crux is a real Comlink handshake or settlement test *running against a spun-up constel* (proving the harness earns its keep), or the Mycelium consensus design read (over TigerBeetle+Mysticeti). The three breach renames (Dimeroll->Dimeroll - .myc->.kres - council sky->constel sky, each cairn-first) and the deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand (gates #1/#5).

- **Constel dev-net ledger r1 LANDED `20260813`** (`active-designing/20260813-030005_constel-devnet-ledger-exploration.md`, self-approved) -- the harness's own named next crux, taken: a real settlement/economic test *running against a spun-up constel*, proving the bench earns its keep. `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger.rye` gives the tree's real Mycelium supply primitive (`mycelium/fold.rye` -- supply as a pure fold over Kumara-signed facts, `Sigma issued - Sigma taxed`, non-negative, an overdraw refusing the whole fold) **a network to belong to**. The crux, fused: **an economic ledger runs from genesis inside the fake net -- its money signed by the net's own settled identity, refused for anyone else -- and it can never run under a name that could be real.** Two promises hold at once: the **quarantine wraps the ledger, not only the net** (`open_ledger` opens only through `spin_up`, which refuses `UnsafeName` before a single genesis -- no code path to a ledger under an @p-shaped name), and **sovereignty is structural** (`admit` refuses any fact whose signer's key is not the genesis galaxy Deed's `keeper` -> `NotSovereign` before the log; `issue`/`tax` mint sovereign-signed facts, the sovereign keypair derived from the same keeper seed that settled genesis, asserted equal to `galaxy.keeper` -- settlement identity gates the Mycelium supply, neither module weakened). Selftest GREEN (5 cases): a law-safe net opens a ledger whose sovereign is the genesis keeper (fresh supply 0) - issue 100 / tax 30 / issue 20 folds to supply 90 over three sovereign facts - an @p-shaped name opens **no** ledger (`UnsafeName`) - a tax of 80 over supply 50 refuses `Overdraw` whole (supply held at 50, log untouched -- Mycelium non-negativity holds in the fake net) - an alien signer's internally-honest fact refuses `NotSovereign` before the log (log stays empty). Witness `tools/constel_net_ledger_witness.rish` GREEN, re-runs the harness (`constel_net`) **and** the Mycelium fold at its door; TAME bans + width clean. Composes `constel_net.spin_up` + `mycelium/fold` public API only, invents no storage, weakens no bound. Demo keeper seeds only -- the Mycelium fold is a supply *model*, not a payment rail (gate #3 untouched), no real key/funds/network/custody (gate #4 untouched; a served ledger reaches the serve gate). **Constel dev-net ledger r2 LANDED `20260813`:** `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger_stars.rye` carries the **third Mycelium fact kind -- `star_reserve`** -- into the sovereign ledger, mirroring the harness's own r2 (a star + planet settling beneath the galaxy): the ledger's *names* grow beside the topology's *points*, every reservation paid out of the net's own supply. `reserve_star` mints a sovereign-signed reservation and admits it; a reservation is **sovereign-only** (`NotSovereign` before the log), **unique** (a second reservation of a held name refuses `StarTaken` whole, supply held), and **drains supply exactly as tax does** (a reservation priced beyond supply refuses `Overdraw`, non-negativity holding). Additive -- r1 grew only a pub reader `signer_is_sovereign_of` (the exact private gate `admit` uses, so one authority test never drifts into two); r1 re-runs GREEN at r2's door. Selftest GREEN (4 cases: issue 100 then reserve vega(30)+rigel(20) drains to 50 - double-vega refuses StarTaken - overpriced refuses Overdraw - alien reservation refuses NotSovereign). Witness `tools/constel_net_ledger_stars_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/funds/network/custody. **Constel dev-net ledger r3 LANDED `20260813`:** `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger_bron.rye` makes the ledger **travel as text** -- `from_ledger` builds a `PortableLedger` (owned name buffer - sovereign key - roster of signed facts) from a live ledger; `render` emits a `format constel-ledger-v1` record (a `net <name>` line - a `sovereign <pk-hex>` line - one `fact <kind> <amount> <star> <sig-hex>` line each); `parse` reads it back and `supply` re-folds the reconstructed facts through the real Mycelium law.
