Witness `tools/mycelium_constel_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- reads true (Constel arc closes):** `mycelium/constel_true.rye` reads a real on-disk `format constel-v1` fixture (`tools/fixtures/constel_roster.bron`, produced reproducibly by `constel_fixture_gen.rye`, `@embedFile`-bound through `mycelium/corpora/`). **Two tools, one answer:** the app derives the roster shape (`<count> <firstkey8>`) **independently of the record text** -- by booting the constellation from its ship names (each key a pure function of its name) -- and reports `5 97f92882`, cross-checked against `constel_roster_truth.awk`'s reading of the same bytes. **The record is the receipt of the real constellation** -- every carried key self-certifies against its name, the parsed roster matches the name-booted one name-for-name and key-for-key, and it reaches quorum on a demo Dag's true order-head; the record re-renders to the embedded bytes (a fixed point), the generator re-emits byte-identical (`REPRODUCIBLE`), a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_constel_true_witness.rish` GREEN. **The Constel agent-doable arc -- r1 named-quorum crux - r2 fault tolerance - r3 travels self-certifying - r4 reads true -- stands complete end to end.** A **served** constellation (ships attesting over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host for any ship reaches gates #2/#4.. A **served** constellation (ships attesting over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host for any ship reaches gates #2/#4.
- **Testament -- a named constellation's verdict travels as an offline certificate -- `20260813` (r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-135126_mycelium-testament-named-quorum-certificate-exploration.md`). The Constel quorum lived and died **in one process** -- nothing carried the constellation's belief out. `chorus_bron` already lets a bare quorum travel and re-verify at a **stated** threshold, yet it carries no roster, so it cannot tell an enrolled ship from a stranger with a valid signature. A **Testament** closes exactly that gap: a portable certificate that *this named constellation, by Byzantine quorum, certified this reading*, verifiable **offline** holding only the certificate and the roster **names**. **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/testament.rye` seals a reading into a certificate carrying the roster names + the sealed Chorus; made **with** the ledger, verified **without** it -- `verify` boots the exact roster from the names alone (each key `SHA-256(name)`), seats the Muster, **derives** the Byzantine threshold from the roster's size, and passes the seal with no Dag. **The teeth (the named binding a bare Chorus cannot enforce):** a stranger ship's valid, agreeing voucher refuses **`NotMember`**, a thin quorum **`BelowQuorum`**, a one-byte name tamper re-boots a different key -> **`NotMember`**. **r2 LANDED -- fault tolerance inside the certificate:** `mycelium/testament_fault.rye` -- a Testament sealed with `f` ships down still names the **whole** roster, so `verify` boots the full constellation, derives the full threshold, and believes the survivors from the names alone (five ships, threshold 4, f 1: one down still meets four); one more down refuses **`BelowQuorum`** at the honest edge; a permanently smaller roster re-derives its own lower threshold and is believed. **r3 LANDED -- the certificate travels, self-proving:** `mycelium/testament_bron.rye` renders to a `format testament-v1` record (one `ship <name>` line each -- the **name only**, key derived; the shared claim once; one `member <node> <sig>` line per voice) and parses back byte-for-byte (a fixed point). Unlike a `chorus-v1` record it states **no threshold** -- `parse` boots the roster, derives the threshold, and re-runs `verify`, so the named binding travels: a stranger's voice refuses `NotMember`, a thin quorum `BelowQuorum`, a tampered ship name `NotMember`, now across the wire. **r4 LANDED -- reads true, the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/testament_true.rye` reads a **real** on-disk fixture (`tools/fixtures/testament_cert.bron`, produced reproducibly by `mycelium/testament_fixture_gen.rye`, `@embedFile`-bound through `mycelium/corpora/`). **Two tools, one answer:** the app derives the shape (`<ship_count> <head8>`) **independently of the record text** -- ship count by booting the roster from names, head by rebuilding the demo Dag -- reporting `5 c2d99dd2`, cross-checked against `testament_cert_truth.awk`; the certificate self-proves on parse, the parsed roster matches the booted one name-for-name, the sealed head is the demo Dag's true order-head, the record is a fixed point, the fixture `REPRODUCIBLE`, a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witnesses `tools/mycelium_testament{,_fault,_bron,_true}_witness.rish` all GREEN, TAME + width clean; composes `testament`+`constel`+`chorus`+`muster`+`voucher` public API only, editing none; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **The Testament arc stands complete end to end -- r1 named-quorum crux - r2 fault tolerance in the certificate - r3 travels self-proving - r4 reads true.** A named constellation's verdict is now a portable, self-proving certificate a keeper reads by hand -- the Lindy artifact the Mycelium consensus season built toward. ## Card habits

- **kg** -- keep going, next mechanical lap. **check-in** -- pause for Keaton's word / design. **send** -- commit - push both remotes - merge. **remember** -- reprint this card. **align** -- walk the compass, reconcile plan with green witnesses. **molt** -- prep a fossil for shed. **debride** -- remove dead history (Keaton's word). **shred** stays RED until circled. remember != send != kg != align.
- **Vocabulary** -- the tree seats **shape**, not Hoon's *mold*. Prefer **git nib**. One clock: `TZ=America/New_York`.
